- **chimeric-contig-classification-and-count-tabulation**: Use when you have a de novo assembled transcriptome (e.g., Trinity contigs) plus a reference transcript set and need to quantify how many chimeric vs. correct vs. unidentified contigs a filter removed. - **chimeric-read-artifact-modeling**: Use when detecting gene fusions from Smart-Seq scRNA-seq FASTQ data where PCR amplification produces artificial chimeric reads that inflate false-positive fusion candidates. - **chimeric-read-classification**: Use when building or evaluating the false-positive filter of a targeted fusion-detection pipeline that must distinguish true fusion reads from artificial chimeric reads in paired-end RNA-seq data.
